System choice: use the first min(10, length) characters as the date text, and do not skip the record. This keeps the skip rules limited to the source's stated rules.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`--since` and `--until` keep records whose start date lies in an inclusive range. The start date is the first 10 characters of `started_at`. The CLI validates the option values before this filter runs.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Start date = first `min(10, len(started_at))` characters of `started_at`.\n- With `--since D`, keep records whose start date is greater than or equal to `D`.\n- With `--until D`, keep records whose start date is less than or equal to `D`.\n- Comparison is byte-wise on `YYYY-MM-DD` text, which orders the same as calendar dates.\n- Options that are not given do not constrain the range.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC filter-date-inclusive-range ===\nIntent: Records on the since and until boundaries are kept; Centralising these keeps every report byte-consistent.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `FormatMinutes(ms int64) string`: `t = (ms + 3000) / 6000` in integer division, written as `t/10`, a period, and `t%10`. For example `95000` ms writes `1.6`.\n- `FormatCost(usd float64) string`: `fmt.Sprintf(\"%.4f\", usd)`.\n- `WriteCSV(w io.Writer, rows [][]string) error`: writes each row with `encoding/csv`, comma-separated, `\\n` line endings.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-format-minutes-rounding ===\nIntent: Minutes are written as tenths of a minute, rounded half up, so 95000 ms writes 1.6.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\ndef format_minutes(ms):\n    t = (ms + 3000) // 6000\n    return f\"{t // 10}.{t % 10}\"\n\nassert format_minutes(95000) == \"1.6\"\nassert format_minutes(0) == \"0.0\"\n=== END AC report-format-minutes-rounding ===\n\n=== AC report-format-cost-four-places ===\nIntent: Cost is written with exactly four decimal places.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nusd = 5.5245\nassert f\"{usd:.4f}\" == \"5.5245\"\nassert f\"{0.0:.4f}\" == \"0.0000\"\n=== END AC report-format-cost-four-places ===\n\n=== END ARTIFACT ===","ttft_ms":17397,"type":"result","duration_ms":17426,"uuid":"0c11e43b-fa11-456d-8d6f-f7733307e7ed","ttft_stream_ms":1608,"time_to_request_ms":84,"first_content_frame_ms":1609,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0,"model_usage_since_last_result":{"claude-haiku-5-5":{"inputTokens":2,"outputTokens":4806,"cacheReadInputTokens":2348,"cacheCreationInputTokens":80160,"webSearchRequests":0,"costUSD":0.01845868,"contextWindow":1000000,"maxOutputTokens":128000,"thinkingTokens":0,"canonicalModel":"claude-haiku-5-5","provider":"firstParty","costBasis":"list"}}}
